# Actor, relationship and readiness ledger

**Bootstrap baseline:** 0 subjects, actor-state rows, relationships and readiness assessments. At GO-U001 closeout six bounded represented subjects, two directional relations and six readiness dispositions were recorded; current additions appear as separate source-bounded rows. These are narrated-experience observations, not externally corroborated biographies or behavioral models.

Read [the entrypoint](../CURRENT_STATE_AND_CORPUS_MAP.md), [method](../00%20Frameworks%20and%20Methods/GENOCIDAL_ORGAN_ANALYTICAL_METHOD.md) and [reconstruction contract in the architecture](../00%20Frameworks%20and%20Methods/GENOCIDAL_ORGAN_SYNTHESIS_ARCHITECTURE.md). This is the single cumulative owner for actor state, directional relationships and local readiness. The claims ledger owns competing interpretations and their revision history; reference its IDs rather than duplicate that history.

## Current identity/frontier register

The following two paragraphs preserve the historical identity register through GO-U004; their unnamed/romanization limits apply at that cutoff. The GO-U005 update follows them.

GO-C001 is now addressed as **シェパード大尉** and **クラヴィス** in the same continuous exchange (E009/E011); the original unnamed GO-U001 row remains historical. GO-C007 is **ウィリアムズ**. GO-C008’s **機上輸送管理担当 / ロードマスター** is a source role, not an invented personal name. GO-C009 remains **標的Ｂ**, distinct from GO-C010, the unnamed former secret-police chief in an earlier mission. GO-C011 is the unnamed doctor whose consequential question is mediated through the hospital memory. Latin spellings and later identities are not supplied. GO-C012 is アレックス, GO-C013 リーランド, and GO-C014 one unnamed killed patrolman from whose body the tag is recovered; other patrolmen remain an unindividuated group, with three disembarking/four reported dead not silently reconciled. Current state is the latest source-bounded row below; original GO-U001 rows retain their time/disclosure limits.

GO-C015 is the unnamed present former brigadier/targetA/claimed defence minister, distinct from C010’s earlier chief. C016 is the checkpoint guard. C017 records the observed town boys as a cohort, not a shared individual psychology. C018 records anonymous town civilian victims, including distinct observed bodies and execution victims without assuming they are the same people. C009 remains unseen targetB; his culture/information office and announced departure are A’s report. At that update, the frontier was complete GO-U004.

At GO-U005, C009 is newly source-named **ジョン・ポール** (John Paul is analyst romanization). C019 is Alex’s unnamed elderly Irish funeral priest; C020 the unnamed pizza courier. C001/C007 are both said to be30 at this domestic scene; C007 has a wife, an unseen ancillary mention. 男やもめ does not establish a dead wife for C001. Prior names/knowledge limits retain their original frontiers.

At GO-U006, C021 is ロックウェル大佐, the previously unnamed boss now identified in the briefing; C022 is the unnamed intelligence undersecretary labeled DIA by the narrator; C023 エリカ・セイルズ represents ユージーン＆クルップス; C024 is recorded captive アフメド・ハッサン・サラード; C025 the offscreen recorded questioner; C026 the CIA woman in a blue suit; C027 one unidentified speaking legislator. These are source-role identities, with no invented official names, translations or cross-person merges. Current frontier is complete GO-U006.

At GO-U007, C028 is the unnamed woman associated with John Paul in Williams’s account; her exact relationship/knowledge is unobserved. C029 is the reported junior CIA embassy officer, not the separate CIA woman C026 from Washington. C030 records the unnamed flight-attendant role (number unspecified). Current frontier is complete GO-U007; dream appearances and waking states remain separated in the rows.

GO-U008 identifies the teacher as **ルツィア・シュクロウプ**. C028 retains the U007 unnamed lead rather than inventing a duplicate: continuity from proposed approach to the current home/teacher encounter strongly supports the referential link, but U008 does not independently restate her relationship to the target. The original mediated C028 row remains historical. Current teaching/conversation, narrated age33 and reported American study now have direct or explicitly attributed channels; source-name recognition is not presented as blind discovery.

At GO-U009, **ビショップ** is explicitly C001’s false-ID name for the advertising-worker persona; conversation about its family does not establish his actual household. C031 is **ドナルド**, a colleague posting the Prague lead; its underlying multistage report remains hearsay. C032 records the **CIA support personnel** participating in surveillance shifts, number unspecified and without a pooled private psychology. C028 calls her former MIT language-research partner John, and the narrator explicitly treats the man whose reading taste he imagines as ジョン・ポール at N119. That source identification establishes the current referent; the underlying relationship/research biography remains attributed testimony. Current frontier is complete GO-U009.

At GO-U010, C033 is the unnamed assaulted young follower; incompatible retinal/fingerprint records supply no verified name or government allegiance. C034 and C035 are John’s unnamed wife and six-year-old daughter, distinctly recognized through the dossier-mediated Sarajevo loss; their private states are unreported. C036 records the two other identified followers (one man/one woman) as a functional pair without a pooled psychology or assumed affiliation. C028’s reported student identity at the historical apartment is distinguished from her present teaching role. Current frontier is complete GO-U010.

At GO-U011, C002mother and C012Alex appear within an explicitly identified nightmare; N74calls their forms the narrator’s own self, not independent posthumous testimony. C037recognizes Williams’s unnamed wife, previously an ancillary mention, now reported as a fellow counseling attendee; C038their unnamed daughter is mentioned in the childcare arrangement; C039the unnamed male military counselor is known only through Williams’s bounded report. No anonymous follower is identified anew. Kafka/family/Ottla/IBM and massacre histories remain embedded historical references, not newly independently sourced fictional actors. Current frontier is complete GO-U011.

At GO-U012, C001’s presented alias is now fully **チャールズ・ビショップ**; actual employment biography remains cover. C040 is source-named **ルーシャス**, introduced as clubowner, a newly introduced actor whose broader role remains unsettled by the narrator’s question. C041 is **タイロン**, present and approached by Lucia; the owner’s sayshemissesher report supplies no direct Tyron speech/privateproof. Anonymousdancers/servingstaff remain scene roles without forced individualpsychology; graphicbrain is explicitly a display. Currentfrontier completeGO-U012.

Direction is explicit. A -> B does not establish B -> A. Separate promised, intended, attempted and completed acts, consent from compliance, social status from private feeling, and perception from corroborated facts. A relation can remain unilateral or unknown.

Model production and validation are separate columns. Full reading coverage does not imply reconstructibility. GO-U001 grades above describe narrow evidence limits; no subject merits an unconstrained model.
